```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { ErrorState } from '@/components/ui/error-state.tsx';
import { Skeleton } from '@/components/ui/skeleton.tsx';
import { LeadVerbs } from '@/features/leads/lead-verbs.tsx';
import { ComposerPlaceholder } from '@/features/timeline/composer-placeholder.tsx';
import { Timeline } from '@/features/timeline/timeline.tsx';
import { useCopyLinkTarget } from '@/lib/copy-link.tsx';
import { useCompanyRecord } from '@/lib/query/use-records.ts';
import { useDelayedFlag } from '@/lib/use-delayed-flag.ts';
import { LeadCard } from './lead-card.tsx';
import { CompanyAttributes } from './record-attributes.tsx';

export function CompanyRecord({ companyId }: { readonly companyId: string }) {
  const record = useCompanyRecord(companyId);
  const loading = useDelayedFlag(record.isPending);
  const [focusedId, setFocusedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
  useCopyLinkTarget(`/companies/${companyId}`);

  if (record.error !== null) {
    return <ErrorState title="Could not load this company" error={record.error} onRetry={() => { record.refetch().catch(() => undefined); }} />;
  }
  if (record.data === undefined) return loading ? <Skeleton className="m-6 h-40" /> : null;
  const { company, people, leads } = record.data;
  const focused = leads.find((lead) => lead.id === focusedId) ?? leads[0];

  return (
    <div className="grid h-full min-h-0 grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-[minmax(320px,420px)_1fr]">
      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col gap-4 overflow-y-auto border-border p-4 lg:border-r">
        <header className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
          <h1 className="font-medium text-lg text-text">{company.name}</h1>
          {company.primaryDomain === null ? null : <p className="text-muted text-dense">{company.primaryDomain}</p>}
        </header>
        <CompanyAttributes company={company} />
        <section aria-label="People" className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <h2 className="font-medium text-2xs text-faint uppercase">People</h2>
          {people.map(({ person, employment }) => (
            <Link key={person.id} href={`/people/${person.id}`} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md px-1 py-1 text-dense text-text">
              <span className="truncate">{person.name}</span>
              <span className="truncate text-muted">{employment.title ?? ''}</span>
              <span className="ml-auto flex gap-1">
                {leads
                  .filter((lead) => lead.personId === person.id)
                  .map((lead) => (
                    <span key={lead.id} className="rounded-sm border border-border px-1 font-mono text-2xs text-faint">
                      {lead.key}
                    </span>
                  ))}
              </span>
            </Link>
          ))}
        </section>
        <section aria-label="Leads" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <h2 className="font-medium text-2xs text-faint uppercase">Leads</h2>
          {leads.map((lead) => (
            <LeadCard key={lead.id} lead={lead} focused={lead.id === focused?.id} onFocus={() => setFocusedId(lead.id)} />
          ))}
        </section>
        {focused === undefined ? null : (
          <LeadVerbs
            selection={{ targets: [focused], active: focused, clear: () => undefined }}
            pipelineId={focused.pipelineId}
            anchorPrefix="lead-card-"
            verbs={['stage', 'owner', 'priority', 'hold', 'close']}
          />
        )}
      </div>
      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-col">
        <Timeline subjectType="company" subjectId={company.id} />
        <ComposerPlaceholder />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/app/(app)/l/[identifier]/page.tsx`:

```tsx
import { getLeadByKey } from '@gravity/core';
import { isDomainError } from '@gravity/shared/errors';
import { notFound, redirect } from 'next/navigation';
import { pageContext } from '@/lib/api/handler.ts';

export default async function LeadKeyPage({ params }: { readonly params: Promise<{ identifier: string }> }) {
  const { principal } = await pageContext();
  const { identifier } = await params;
  let target: string;
  try {
    const lead = await getLeadByKey(principal, decodeURIComponent(identifier));
    target = `/people/${lead.personId}?lead=${lead.id}`;
  } catch (error: unknown) {
    if (isDomainError(error) && error.code === 'not_found') notFound();
    throw error;
  }
  redirect(target);
}
```

### Task 19: Quick create and palette record search

Adapts `$ORBIT_SRC/apps/web/src/features/issues/quick-create*` (a dialog opened with `C`, `Cmd+Enter` to create) and Orbit's palette search (`$ORBIT_SRC/apps/web/src/lib/query/use-issue-search.ts`, local results first, server results merged). Local matches come from every cached list and record, so a person already on screen shows instantly; the server adds what the cache lacks within 140ms of the last keystroke.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/features/quick-create/quick-create-form.ts`, `apps/web/src/features/quick-create/quick-create-dialog.tsx`, `apps/web/src/lib/query/record-search.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/query/use-record-search.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/lib/query/keys.ts` (add `DUPLICATES_ROOT` and `queryKeys.duplicates(probe)`), `apps/web/src/components/layout/app-shell.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/command-palette.tsx`
- Test: `apps/web/tests/features/quick-create/quick-create-form.test.ts`, `apps/web/tests/lib/query/record-search.test.ts`, `apps/web/tests/features/quick-create/quick-create-dialog.test.tsx`, `apps/web/tests/components/command-palette-search.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `parseIdentityInput`, `companyDomainFromEmail`, `parseLeadKey`, `quickCreateSchema`, `randomUUIDv7` (Task 1); `allCachedLeads`, `allCachedPeople`, `allCachedCompanies`, `useQuickCreateLead` (Task 11); `useDebouncedValue` (Task 16); `lastPipelineKey` (Task 13); `personHref` (Task 14).
- Produces:
  - `QuickCreateDraft`, `BuiltQuickCreate = { body: QuickCreateBody; preview: LeadRow }` (with `QuickCreateBody = z.input<typeof quickCreateSchema> & { leadId: string }` from `use-lead-mutations.ts`), `buildQuickCreate(draft, context): BuiltQuickCreate | { error: string }`.
  - `PersonHit = { id: string; name: string; email: string | null; companyName: string | null; linkedinUrl: string | null }`, `RecordProbe = { email: string | null; linkedinUrl: string | null; name: string | null }`, `LocalHits = { people: PersonHit[]; companies: CompanyRow[]; leads: LeadRow[] }`, `searchCachedRecords(client, term, limit?)`, `matchCachedPeople(client, probe)`.
  - `useRecordSearch(term)`, `useDuplicates(probe)`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { buildQuickCreate } from '@/features/quick-create/quick-create-form.ts';
import { bootstrapFixture } from '../../support/bootstrap-fixture.ts';

const bootstrap = bootstrapFixture();
const pipeline = bootstrap.pipelines[0];
if (pipeline === undefined) throw new Error('fixture pipeline');
const context = { leadId: '0192f0a0-0000-7000-8000-000000000001', pipeline, stages: bootstrap.stages, organizationId: 'o1', now: new Date('2026-10-03T10:00:00.000Z') };

describe('buildQuickCreate', () => {
  test('an email fills the person and the company comes from its domain', () => {
    const built = buildQuickCreate(
      { identity: 'Grace@Navy.mil', name: 'Grace Hopper', companyName: '', companyDomain: 'navy.mil', pipelineId: 'p1', ownerId: 'u1' },
      context,
    );
    if ('error' in built) throw new Error(built.error);
    expect(built.body).toEqual({
      leadId: context.leadId,
      pipelineId: 'p1',
      ownerId: 'u1',
      person: { name: 'Grace Hopper', emails: ['grace@navy.mil'], linkedinUrl: null, company: { domain: 'navy.mil' } },
    });
    expect(built.preview).toMatchObject({ id: context.leadId, key: 'YOD-new', stageId: 'new', personName: 'Grace Hopper', companyName: 'navy.mil', syncId: 0 });
  });

  test('a bare name works and an email without a name uses its local part', () => {
    const named = buildQuickCreate({ identity: 'Linus Torvalds', name: '', companyName: 'Initech', companyDomain: '', pipelineId: 'p1', ownerId: null }, context);
    if ('error' in named) throw new Error(named.error);
    expect(named.body.person).toEqual({ name: 'Linus Torvalds', emails: [], linkedinUrl: null, company: { name: 'Initech' } });
    const local = buildQuickCreate({ identity: 'ada@acme.io', name: '', companyName: '', companyDomain: '', pipelineId: 'p1', ownerId: null }, context);
    if ('error' in local) throw new Error(local.error);
    expect(local.body.person.name).toBe('ada');
  });

  test('refuses an empty draft', () => {
    expect(buildQuickCreate({ identity: ' ', name: '', companyName: '', companyDomain: '', pipelineId: 'p1', ownerId: null }, context)).toEqual({
      error: 'Add a name, an email or a LinkedIn URL.',
    });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { emptyFilterGroup, encodeListQuery } from '@gravity/shared/filters';
import { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { queryKeys } from '@/lib/query/keys.ts';
import { matchCachedPeople, searchCachedRecords } from '@/lib/query/record-search.ts';
import { leadFixture } from '../../support/lead-fixture.ts';

function seeded(): QueryClient {
  const client = new QueryClient();
  client.setQueryData(queryKeys.leads('p1', encodeListQuery({ filter: emptyFilterGroup(), q: '' })), {
    pages: [
      {
        leads: [
          leadFixture({ id: 'l1', key: 'YOD-1', personId: 'per1', personName: 'Ada Lovelace', personEmail: 'ada@acme.io', personLinkedinUrl: 'https://www.linkedin.com/in/ada' }),
          leadFixture({ id: 'l12', key: 'YOD-12', personId: 'per2', personName: 'Grace Hopper', personEmail: 'grace@navy.mil' }),
        ],
        nextCursor: null,
      },
    ],
    pageParams: [null],
  });
  return client;
}

describe('searchCachedRecords', () => {
  test('finds people from cached lead rows and leads by key', () => {
    const hits = searchCachedRecords(seeded(), 'acme');
    expect(hits.people.map((person) => person.name)).toEqual(['Ada Lovelace']);
    expect(searchCachedRecords(seeded(), 'yod-12').leads.map((lead) => lead.key)).toEqual(['YOD-12']);
  });
});

describe('matchCachedPeople', () => {
  test('matches exactly by email, LinkedIn URL or name', () => {
    const client = seeded();
    expect(matchCachedPeople(client, { email: 'ADA@acme.io', linkedinUrl: null, name: null }).map((person) => person.id)).toEqual(['per1']);
    expect(matchCachedPeople(client, { email: null, linkedinUrl: 'https://www.linkedin.com/in/ada', name: null })).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(matchCachedPeople(client, { email: null, linkedinUrl: null, name: 'grace hopper' }).map((person) => person.id)).toEqual(['per2']);
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { afterEach, describe, expect, test } from 'bun:test';
import { emptyFilterGroup, encodeListQuery } from '@gravity/shared/filters';
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { cachedLead, allCachedLeads } from '@/lib/query/lead-cache.ts';
import { queryKeys } from '@/lib/query/keys.ts';
import { restoreModulesAfterThisFile } from '../../../tests-support.ts';
import { serveJson } from '../../support/fetch.ts';
import { leadFixture } from '../../support/lead-fixture.ts';
import { mockNavigation } from '../../support/navigation.ts';
import { renderWithClient } from '../../support/render.tsx';

await restoreModulesAfterThisFile(['next/navigation']);
mockNavigation('/leads/YOD');

const { QuickCreate } = await import('@/features/quick-create/quick-create-dialog.tsx');

const realFetch = globalThis.fetch;
afterEach(() => {
  globalThis.fetch = realFetch;
});

const listKey = queryKeys.leads('p1', encodeListQuery({ filter: emptyFilterGroup(), q: '' }));

function renderQuickCreate() {
  const result = renderWithClient(<QuickCreate />);
  result.client.setQueryData(listKey, {
    pages: [{ leads: [leadFixture({ personName: 'Ada Lovelace', personEmail: 'ada@acme.io' })], nextCursor: null }],
    pageParams: [null],
  });
  return result;
}

describe('QuickCreate', () => {
  test('C opens it and a known email shows the existing person before the server answers', async () => {
    serveJson(() => ({ body: { people: [], companies: [] } }));
    renderQuickCreate();
    await userEvent.keyboard('c');
    await userEvent.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Person'), 'ada@acme.io');
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Open Ada Lovelace/ })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByLabelText('Company domain')).toHaveValue('acme.io');
  });

  test('Cmd+Enter creates the person and lead and shows the row at once', async () => {
    const sent = serveJson((url) => {
      if (url.pathname === '/api/duplicates') return { body: { people: [], companies: [] } };
      return { status: 500, body: { error: { code: 'internal', message: 'held for the test' } } };
    });
    const { client } = renderQuickCreate();
    await userEvent.keyboard('c');
    await userEvent.type(await screen.findByLabelText('Person'), 'grace@navy.mil');
    await userEvent.type(screen.getByLabelText('Name'), 'Grace Hopper');
    await userEvent.keyboard('{Meta>}{Enter}{/Meta}');
    expect(allCachedLeads(client).some((lead) => lead.personName === 'Grace Hopper' && lead.key === 'YOD-new')).toBe(true);
    await waitFor(() => expect(sent.some((entry) => entry.url === '/api/leads/quick')).toBe(true));
    const body = sent.find((entry) => entry.url === '/api/leads/quick')?.body as { person: unknown; pipelineId: string; leadId: string };
    expect(body).toMatchObject({ pipelineId: 'p1', person: { name: 'Grace Hopper', emails: ['grace@navy.mil'], company: { domain: 'navy.mil' } } });
    await waitFor(() => expect(cachedLead(client, body.leadId)).toBeUndefined());
  });
});
```

The second test answers the create with 500 to prove both halves: the optimistic row appears before any response and is removed on failure.

`apps/web/src/features/quick-create/quick-create-form.ts`:

```ts
import type { LeadRow, PipelineRow, StageRow } from '@gravity/shared/records';
import { type IdentityInput, parseIdentityInput } from '@gravity/shared/utils';
import type { CompanyRef } from '@gravity/shared/validators';
import type { QuickCreateBody } from '@/lib/query/use-lead-mutations.ts';

export interface QuickCreateDraft {
  readonly identity: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly companyName: string;
  readonly companyDomain: string;
  readonly pipelineId: string;
  readonly ownerId: string | null;
}

export interface QuickCreateContext {
  readonly leadId: string;
  readonly pipeline: PipelineRow;
  readonly stages: readonly StageRow[];
  readonly organizationId: string;
  readonly now: Date;
}

export interface BuiltQuickCreate {
  readonly body: QuickCreateBody;
  readonly preview: LeadRow;
}

function companyOf(draft: QuickCreateDraft): CompanyRef | null {
  const domain = draft.companyDomain.trim();
  const name = draft.companyName.trim();
  if (domain.length > 0) return name.length > 0 ? { domain, name } : { domain };
  return name.length > 0 ? { name } : null;
}

function companyLabel(company: CompanyRef | null): string | null {
  if (company === null || 'id' in company) return null;
  if ('domain' in company) return company.name ?? company.domain;
  return company.name;
}

function personName(identity: IdentityInput, typed: string): string {
  if (identity.kind === 'name') return identity.name;
  if (typed.length > 0) return typed;
  if (identity.kind === 'email') return identity.email.split('@')[0] ?? '';
  return identity.linkedinUrl.split('/in/')[1] ?? '';
}

function firstOpenStage(stages: readonly StageRow[], pipelineId: string): StageRow | undefined {
  return stages
    .filter((stage) => stage.pipelineId === pipelineId && stage.archivedAt === null && stage.category === 'open')
    .sort((a, b) => a.sortOrder - b.sortOrder)[0];
}

export function buildQuickCreate(draft: QuickCreateDraft, context: QuickCreateContext): BuiltQuickCreate | { error: string } {
  const identity = parseIdentityInput(draft.identity);
  if (identity === null) return { error: 'Add a name, an email or a LinkedIn URL.' };
  const name = personName(identity, draft.name.trim());
  if (name.length === 0) return { error: 'Add a name, an email or a LinkedIn URL.' };
  const stage = firstOpenStage(context.stages, context.pipeline.id);
  if (stage === undefined) return { error: 'This pipeline has no open stage. Add one in Settings.' };
  const email = identity.kind === 'email' ? identity.email : null;
  const linkedinUrl = identity.kind === 'linkedin' ? identity.linkedinUrl : null;
  const company = companyOf(draft);
  const at = context.now.toISOString();
  return {
    body: {
      leadId: context.leadId,
      pipelineId: draft.pipelineId,
      ownerId: draft.ownerId,
      person: { name, emails: email === null ? [] : [email], linkedinUrl, company },
    },
    preview: {
      id: context.leadId,
      organizationId: context.organizationId,
      pipelineId: context.pipeline.id,
      brandId: context.pipeline.brandId,
      number: 0,
      key: `${context.pipeline.key}-new`,
      personId: `pending-${context.leadId}`,
      personName: name,
      personEmail: email,
      personLinkedinUrl: linkedinUrl,
      companyId: null,
      companyName: companyLabel(company),
      ownerId: draft.ownerId,
      stageId: stage.id,
      stageCategory: 'open',
      source: 'manual',
      priority: 0,
      holdReason: null,
      holdUntil: null,
      nextAction: null,
      nextActionAt: null,
      owedBy: 'none',
      lastInboundAt: null,
      lastOutboundAt: null,
      fields: {},
      syncId: 0,
      createdAt: at,
      updatedAt: at,
      archivedAt: null,
    },
  };
}
```

`IdentityInput` is exported from `packages/shared/src/utils/identity.ts` (Task 1).

`apps/web/src/lib/query/record-search.ts`:

```ts
import { searchTokens } from '@gravity/shared/filters';
import type { CompanyRow, LeadRow } from '@gravity/shared/records';
import type { QueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { allCachedLeads } from './lead-cache.ts';
import { allCachedCompanies, allCachedPeople } from './record-cache.ts';

export interface PersonHit {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly email: string | null;
  readonly companyName: string | null;
  readonly linkedinUrl: string | null;
}

export interface RecordProbe {
  readonly email: string | null;
  readonly linkedinUrl: string | null;
  readonly name: string | null;
}

export interface LocalHits {
  readonly people: PersonHit[];
  readonly companies: CompanyRow[];
  readonly leads: LeadRow[];
}

export const EMPTY_HITS: LocalHits = { people: [], companies: [], leads: [] };

function cachedPeople(client: QueryClient): PersonHit[] {
  const byId = new Map<string, PersonHit>();
  for (const person of allCachedPeople(client)) {
    byId.set(person.id, { id: person.id, name: person.name, email: person.primaryEmail, companyName: person.companyName, linkedinUrl: person.linkedinUrl });
  }
  for (const lead of allCachedLeads(client)) {
    if (byId.has(lead.personId) || lead.personId.startsWith('pending-')) continue;
    byId.set(lead.personId, { id: lead.personId, name: lead.personName, email: lead.personEmail, companyName: lead.companyName, linkedinUrl: lead.personLinkedinUrl });
  }
  return [...byId.values()];
}

function matches(fields: readonly (string | null)[], tokens: readonly string[]): boolean {
  const haystack = fields.filter((field): field is string => field !== null).map((field) => field.toLowerCase());
  return tokens.every((token) => haystack.some((field) => field.includes(token)));
}

export function searchCachedRecords(client: QueryClient, term: string, limit = 8): LocalHits {
  const tokens = searchTokens(term);
  if (tokens.length === 0) return EMPTY_HITS;
  return {
    people: cachedPeople(client).filter((person) => matches([person.name, person.email, person.companyName], tokens)).slice(0, limit),
    companies: allCachedCompanies(client).filter((company) => matches([company.name, company.primaryDomain], tokens)).slice(0, limit),
    leads: allCachedLeads(client).filter((lead) => matches([lead.key, lead.personName], tokens)).slice(0, limit),
  };
}

export function matchCachedPeople(client: QueryClient, probe: RecordProbe): PersonHit[] {
  const email = probe.email?.toLowerCase() ?? null;
  const name = probe.name?.trim().toLowerCase() ?? null;
  return cachedPeople(client).filter(
    (person) =>
      (email !== null && person.email?.toLowerCase() === email) ||
      (probe.linkedinUrl !== null && person.linkedinUrl === probe.linkedinUrl) ||
      (name !== null && name.length > 0 && person.name.toLowerCase() === name),
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/query/use-record-search.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { useQuery } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useDebouncedValue } from '@/lib/use-debounced-value.ts';
import { apiFetch } from './fetcher.ts';
import { queryKeys } from './keys.ts';
import type { RecordProbe } from './record-search.ts';
import { duplicatesSchema, searchResultSchema } from './schemas.ts';

export function useRecordSearch(term: string) {
  const settled = useDebouncedValue(term.trim());
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.search(settled),
    enabled: settled.length >= 2,
    staleTime: 30_000,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => apiFetch(`/api/search?q=${encodeURIComponent(settled)}`, searchResultSchema, { signal }),
  });
}

function probeSearch(probe: RecordProbe): string {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
  if (probe.email !== null) params.set('email', probe.email);
  if (probe.linkedinUrl !== null) params.set('linkedinUrl', probe.linkedinUrl);
  if (probe.name !== null && probe.name.trim().length >= 2) params.set('name', probe.name.trim());
  return params.toString();
}

export function useDuplicates(probe: RecordProbe) {
  const settled = useDebouncedValue(probeSearch(probe));
  return useQuery({
    queryKey: queryKeys.duplicates(settled),
    enabled: settled.length > 0,
    staleTime: 30_000,
    queryFn: ({ signal }) => apiFetch(`/api/duplicates?${settled}`, duplicatesSchema, { signal }),
  });
}
```

In `keys.ts` add `export const DUPLICATES_ROOT = 'duplicates';` and `duplicates: (probe: string) => [DUPLICATES_ROOT, probe] as const,` to `queryKeys`.

`apps/web/src/features/quick-create/quick-create-dialog.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { companyDomainFromEmail, parseIdentityInput, randomUUIDv7 } from '@gravity/shared/utils';
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { usePathname, useRouter } from 'next/navigation';
import { useId, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button.tsx';
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog.tsx';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input.tsx';
import { Kbd } from '@/components/ui/kbd.tsx';
import { useWorkspace } from '@/features/workspace/use-workspace.ts';
import { useHotkey } from '@/lib/keyboard/index.ts';
import { lastPipelineKey } from '@/lib/last-pipeline.ts';
import { matchCachedPeople, type PersonHit } from '@/lib/query/record-search.ts';
import { useBootstrap } from '@/lib/query/use-bootstrap.ts';
import { useQuickCreateLead } from '@/lib/query/use-lead-mutations.ts';
import { useDuplicates } from '@/lib/query/use-record-search.ts';
import { buildQuickCreate } from './quick-create-form.ts';

function mergeHits(first: readonly PersonHit[], second: readonly PersonHit[]): PersonHit[] {
  const seen = new Set(first.map((person) => person.id));
  return [...first, ...second.filter((person) => !seen.has(person.id))];
}

function probeNameOf(parsed: ReturnType<typeof parseIdentityInput>, typed: string): string | null {
  if (parsed?.kind === 'name') return parsed.name;
  const trimmed = typed.trim();
  return trimmed.length > 0 ? trimmed : null;
}

function QuickCreateDialog({ onClose }: { readonly onClose: () => void }) {
  const workspace = useWorkspace();
  const bootstrap = useBootstrap().data;
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const router = useRouter();
  const client = useQueryClient();
  const create = useQuickCreateLead();
  const ids = { pipeline: useId(), owner: useId() };
  const routeKey = pathname.startsWith('/leads/') ? (pathname.split('/')[2] ?? '') : '';
  const initialPipeline =
    workspace.pipelineByKey.get(routeKey) ?? workspace.pipelineByKey.get(lastPipelineKey(workspace.userId) ?? '') ?? workspace.pipelines[0];
  const [identity, setIdentity] = useState('');
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [companyName, setCompanyName] = useState('');
  const [companyDomain, setCompanyDomain] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pipelineId, setPipelineId] = useState(initialPipeline?.id ?? '');
  const [ownerId, setOwnerId] = useState<string>(workspace.userId);
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  const parsed = parseIdentityInput(identity);
  const email = parsed?.kind === 'email' ? parsed.email : null;
  const linkedinUrl = parsed?.kind === 'linkedin' ? parsed.linkedinUrl : null;
  const domain = companyDomain ?? (email === null ? '' : (companyDomainFromEmail(email) ?? ''));
  const probeName = probeNameOf(parsed, name);
  const probe = useMemo(() => ({ email, linkedinUrl, name: probeName }), [email, linkedinUrl, probeName]);
  const local = useMemo(() => matchCachedPeople(client, probe), [client, probe]);
  const remote = useDuplicates(probe);
  const matches = mergeHits(local, (remote.data?.people ?? []).map((person) => ({ id: person.id, name: person.name, email: person.primaryEmail, companyName: person.companyName, linkedinUrl: person.linkedinUrl })));

  const submit = () => {
    const pipeline = workspace.pipelineById.get(pipelineId);
    if (pipeline === undefined || bootstrap === undefined) {
      setError('Choose a pipeline.');
      return;
    }
    const built = buildQuickCreate(
      { identity, name, companyName, companyDomain: domain, pipelineId, ownerId: ownerId === '' ? null : ownerId },
      { leadId: randomUUIDv7(), pipeline, stages: bootstrap.stages, organizationId: bootstrap.organization.id, now: new Date() },
    );
    if ('error' in built) {
      setError(built.error);
      return;
    }
    create.mutate(built);
    onClose();
  };
  useHotkey('mod+enter', submit, { label: 'Create', section: 'General', allowInInput: true, priority: 2 });

  return (
    <Dialog open onOpenChange={(next) => { if (!next) onClose(); }}>
      <DialogContent aria-describedby={undefined} data-testid="quick-create">
        <form
          className="flex flex-col gap-3"
          onSubmit={(event) => {
            event.preventDefault();
            submit();
          }}
        >
          <DialogHeader>
            <DialogTitle>Add a person</DialogTitle>
          </DialogHeader>
          <Input autoFocus aria-label="Person" placeholder="Paste a LinkedIn URL, an email or a name" value={identity} onChange={(event) => setIdentity(event.target.value)} />
          {parsed !== null && parsed.kind !== 'name' ? (
            <Input aria-label="Name" placeholder="Full name" value={name} onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)} />
          ) : null}
          {matches.length === 0 ? null : (
            <section aria-label="Already in Gravity" className="flex flex-col gap-1 rounded-md border border-border p-2">
              <h3 className="text-faint text-xs">Already in Gravity</h3>
              {matches.map((person) => (
                <Button
                  key={person.id}
                  type="button"
                  variant="ghost"
                  size="sm"
                  className="justify-start"
                  aria-label={`Open ${person.name}`}
                  onClick={() => {
                    onClose();
                    router.push(`/people/${person.id}`);
                  }}
                >
                  {person.name}
                  <span className="text-faint">{person.email ?? person.companyName ?? ''}</span>
                </Button>
              ))}
            </section>
          )}
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
            <Input aria-label="Company" placeholder="Company" value={companyName} onChange={(event) => setCompanyName(event.target.value)} />
            <Input aria-label="Company domain" placeholder="acme.com" value={domain} onChange={(event) => setCompanyDomain(event.target.value)} />
          </div>
          <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-dense">
            <label htmlFor={ids.pipeline} className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-faint text-xs">
              Pipeline
              <select id={ids.pipeline} value={pipelineId} onChange={(event) => setPipelineId(event.target.value)} className="h-8 rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-dense text-text">
                {workspace.pipelines.map((pipeline) => (
                  <option key={pipeline.id} value={pipeline.id}>
                    {workspace.brandById.get(pipeline.brandId)?.name ?? ''} · {pipeline.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
            <label htmlFor={ids.owner} className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-faint text-xs">
              Owner
              <select id={ids.owner} value={ownerId} onChange={(event) => setOwnerId(event.target.value)} className="h-8 rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-dense text-text">
                <option value="">Unassigned</option>
                {workspace.members.map((member) => (
                  <option key={member.userId} value={member.userId}>
                    {member.name}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>
            </label>
          </div>
          {error === null ? null : <p role="alert" className="text-danger text-xs">{error}</p>}
          <DialogFooter>
            <Button type="submit" variant="primary">
              Create <Kbd keys={['mod', 'enter']} />
            </Button>
          </DialogFooter>
        </form>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  );
}

export function QuickCreate() {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  useHotkey('c', () => setOpen(true), { label: 'Quick create', section: 'General' });
  return open ? <QuickCreateDialog onClose={() => setOpen(false)} /> : null;
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add record search to the palette**

In `apps/web/src/components/command-palette.tsx`:

- Change the input placeholder to `Type a command or search`.
- Read the cache and the server inside `CommandPalette`:

```tsx
const client = useQueryClient();
const local = useMemo(() => (open ? searchCachedRecords(client, term) : EMPTY_HITS), [client, term, open]);
const remote = useRecordSearch(term);
const people = mergeHits(local.people, (remote.data?.people ?? []).map(toPersonHit));
const companies = mergeById(local.companies, remote.data?.companies ?? []);
const leads = mergeById(local.leads, remote.data?.leads ?? []);
const leadKey = parseLeadKey(term);
```

with two small helpers in the same file:

```ts
function mergeById<T extends { readonly id: string }>(first: readonly T[], second: readonly T[]): T[] {
  const seen = new Set(first.map((row) => row.id));
  return [...first, ...second.filter((row) => !seen.has(row.id))].slice(0, 8);
}

function toPersonHit(person: PersonRow): PersonHit {
  return { id: person.id, name: person.name, email: person.primaryEmail, companyName: person.companyName, linkedinUrl: person.linkedinUrl };
}
```

and `mergeHits` as `mergeById` over `PersonHit`.

- Render a `Records` group above the command groups when there is a lead key or any hit; every record item uses `forceMount` so cmdk's own filter never hides a server match that is fuzzy:

```tsx
{leadKey === null && people.length + companies.length + leads.length === 0 ? null : (
  <Command.Group heading="Records" className={groupClassName}>
    {leadKey === null ? null : (
      <Command.Item forceMount value={`open-${leadKey.key}-${leadKey.number}`} className={itemClassName} onSelect={() => go(`/l/${leadKey.key}-${leadKey.number}`)}>
        <ArrowRight className="size-4 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.75} aria-hidden="true" />
        <span className="flex-1 truncate">Open {leadKey.key}-{leadKey.number}</span>
      </Command.Item>
    )}
    {people.map((person) => (
      <Command.Item key={person.id} forceMount value={`person-${person.id}`} className={itemClassName} onSelect={() => go(`/people/${person.id}`)}>
        <User className="size-4 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.75} aria-hidden="true" />
        <span className="flex-1 truncate">{person.name}</span>
        <span className="truncate text-faint text-xs">{person.email ?? person.companyName ?? ''}</span>
      </Command.Item>
    ))}
    {companies.map((company) => (
      <Command.Item key={company.id} forceMount value={`company-${company.id}`} className={itemClassName} onSelect={() => go(`/companies/${company.id}`)}>
        <Building2 className="size-4 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.75} aria-hidden="true" />
        <span className="flex-1 truncate">{company.name}</span>
        <span className="truncate text-faint text-xs">{company.primaryDomain ?? ''}</span>
      </Command.Item>
    ))}
    {leads.map((lead) => (
      <Command.Item key={lead.id} forceMount value={`lead-${lead.id}`} className={itemClassName} onSelect={() => go(personHref(lead))}>
        <Target className="size-4 shrink-0" strokeWidth={1.75} aria-hidden="true" />
        <span className="flex-1 truncate">
          {lead.key} {lead.personName}
        </span>
      </Command.Item>
    ))}
  </Command.Group>
)}
```

where `const go = (href: string) => { onOpenChange(false); setTerm(''); router.push(href); };`. Import `Building2`, `Target` and `User` from `lucide-react`, `parseLeadKey` from `@gravity/shared/utils`, `PersonRow` from `@gravity/shared/records`, `useQueryClient` from `@tanstack/react-query`, `EMPTY_HITS`, `PersonHit` and `searchCachedRecords` from `@/lib/query/record-search.ts`, `useRecordSearch` from `@/lib/query/use-record-search.ts`, and `personHref` from `@/features/leads/lead-groups.ts`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the helpers and the configuration mutations**

`apps/web/src/features/settings/field-key.ts`:

```ts
const MAX_KEY = 40;

function slug(label: string, separator: string): string {
  const pattern = separator === '_' ? /_+/g : /-+/g;
  return label
    .normalize('NFKD')
    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, '')
    .toLowerCase()
    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, separator)
    .replace(pattern, separator)
    .replace(new RegExp(`^${separator}|${separator}$`, 'g'), '');
}

export function fieldKeyFromLabel(label: string): string {
  const key = slug(label, '_');
  if (key.length === 0) return 'field';
  const prefixed = /^[a-z]/.test(key) ? key : `field_${key}`;
  return prefixed.slice(0, MAX_KEY).replace(/_+$/, '');
}

export function optionsFromLines(text: string): { value: string; label: string }[] {
  const seen = new Set<string>();
  const options: { value: string; label: string }[] = [];
  for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    const label = line.trim();
    const value = slug(label, '-').slice(0, 64);
    if (label.length === 0 || value.length === 0 || seen.has(value)) continue;
    seen.add(value);
    options.push({ value, label });
  }
  return options;
}

export function movedIds(ids: readonly string[], index: number, direction: -1 | 1): string[] {
  const next = [...ids];
  const target = index + direction;
  const here = next[index];
  const there = next[target];
  if (here === undefined || there === undefined) return next;
  next[index] = there;
  next[target] = here;
  return next;
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/workspace/use-can.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import { can, type Permission } from '@gravity/shared/policy';
import { useBootstrap } from '@/lib/query/use-bootstrap.ts';

export function useCan(permission: Permission): boolean {
  const { data } = useBootstrap();
  if (data === undefined) return false;
  return can({ userId: data.me.userId, organizationId: data.organization.id, role: data.me.role }, permission);
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/query/use-config-mutations.ts`:

```ts
'use client';

import type { BrandColor, FieldObject, FieldType, PipelineKind, StageCategory } from '@gravity/shared/constants';
import type { BrandRow, FieldDefinitionRow, PipelineRow, StageRow } from '@gravity/shared/records';
import { upsertById, withoutId } from './bootstrap-cache.ts';
import { apiFetch } from './fetcher.ts';
import {
  type Bootstrap,
  brandCreatedSchema,
  brandEnvelopeSchema,
  fieldEnvelopeSchema,
  pipelineCreatedSchema,
  pipelineEnvelopeSchema,
  stageEnvelopeSchema,
  stagesEnvelopeSchema,
} from './schemas.ts';
import { useBootstrapMutation } from './use-bootstrap-mutation.ts';

function upsertAll<T extends { readonly id: string }>(list: readonly T[], rows: readonly T[]): T[] {
  const ids = new Set(rows.map((row) => row.id));
  return [...list.filter((row) => !ids.has(row.id)), ...rows];
}

function withoutPipeline(bootstrap: Bootstrap, pipelineId: string): Bootstrap {
  return {
    ...bootstrap,
    pipelines: withoutId(bootstrap.pipelines, pipelineId),
    stages: bootstrap.stages.filter((stage) => stage.pipelineId !== pipelineId),
  };
}

export interface BrandDraft {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly domain: string | null;
  readonly color: BrandColor;
  readonly pipelineKey: string;
}

export function useCreateBrand() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: BrandDraft) => apiFetch('/api/brands', brandCreatedSchema, { method: 'POST', body: input }),
    settle: (bootstrap, result) => ({
      ...bootstrap,
      brands: upsertById(bootstrap.brands, result.brand),
      pipelines: upsertById(bootstrap.pipelines, result.pipeline),
      stages: upsertAll(bootstrap.stages, result.stages),
    }),
    failure: (input) => `Could not create ${input.name}`,
  });
}

export interface BrandEdit {
  readonly brand: BrandRow;
  readonly patch: Partial<Pick<BrandRow, 'name' | 'domain' | 'color' | 'signature'>>;
}

export function useUpdateBrand() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ brand, patch }: BrandEdit) =>
      (await apiFetch(`/api/brands/${brand.id}`, brandEnvelopeSchema, { method: 'PATCH', body: patch })).brand,
    optimistic: (bootstrap, { brand, patch }) => ({ ...bootstrap, brands: upsertById(bootstrap.brands, { ...brand, ...patch }) }),
    settle: (bootstrap, brand) => ({ ...bootstrap, brands: upsertById(bootstrap.brands, brand) }),
    failure: ({ brand }) => `Could not update ${brand.name}`,
  });
}

export function useArchiveBrand() {
  const remove = (bootstrap: Bootstrap, brand: BrandRow): Bootstrap => {
    const pipelineIds = new Set(bootstrap.pipelines.filter((pipeline) => pipeline.brandId === brand.id).map((pipeline) => pipeline.id));
    return {
      ...bootstrap,
      brands: withoutId(bootstrap.brands, brand.id),
      pipelines: bootstrap.pipelines.filter((pipeline) => !pipelineIds.has(pipeline.id)),
      stages: bootstrap.stages.filter((stage) => !pipelineIds.has(stage.pipelineId)),
    };
  };
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: async (brand: BrandRow) => (await apiFetch(`/api/brands/${brand.id}`, brandEnvelopeSchema, { method: 'DELETE' })).brand,
    optimistic: remove,
    settle: remove,
    failure: (brand) => `Could not archive ${brand.name}`,
  });
}

export interface PipelineDraft {
  readonly brandId: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly key: string;
  readonly kind: PipelineKind;
}

export function useCreatePipeline() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: (input: PipelineDraft) => apiFetch('/api/pipelines', pipelineCreatedSchema, { method: 'POST', body: input }),
    settle: (bootstrap, result) => ({
      ...bootstrap,
      pipelines: upsertById(bootstrap.pipelines, result.pipeline),
      stages: upsertAll(bootstrap.stages, result.stages),
    }),
    failure: (input) => `Could not create ${input.name}`,
  });
}

export interface PipelineEdit {
  readonly pipeline: PipelineRow;
  readonly patch: Partial<Pick<PipelineRow, 'name' | 'key'>>;
}

export function useUpdatePipeline() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ pipeline, patch }: PipelineEdit) =>
      (await apiFetch(`/api/pipelines/${pipeline.id}`, pipelineEnvelopeSchema, { method: 'PATCH', body: patch })).pipeline,
    optimistic: (bootstrap, { pipeline, patch }) => ({ ...bootstrap, pipelines: upsertById(bootstrap.pipelines, { ...pipeline, ...patch }) }),
    settle: (bootstrap, pipeline) => ({ ...bootstrap, pipelines: upsertById(bootstrap.pipelines, pipeline) }),
    failure: ({ pipeline }) => `Could not update ${pipeline.name}`,
  });
}

export function useArchivePipeline() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: async (pipeline: PipelineRow) =>
      (await apiFetch(`/api/pipelines/${pipeline.id}`, pipelineEnvelopeSchema, { method: 'DELETE' })).pipeline,
    optimistic: (bootstrap, pipeline) => withoutPipeline(bootstrap, pipeline.id),
    settle: (bootstrap, pipeline) => withoutPipeline(bootstrap, pipeline.id),
    failure: (pipeline) => `Could not archive ${pipeline.name}`,
  });
}

export interface StageDraft {
  readonly pipelineId: string;
  readonly name: string;
  readonly category: StageCategory;
}

export function useCreateStage() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: StageDraft) => (await apiFetch('/api/stages', stageEnvelopeSchema, { method: 'POST', body: input })).stage,
    settle: (bootstrap, stage) => ({ ...bootstrap, stages: upsertById(bootstrap.stages, stage) }),
    failure: (input) => `Could not add ${input.name}`,
  });
}

export interface StageEdit {
  readonly stage: StageRow;
  readonly patch: Partial<Pick<StageRow, 'name' | 'category'>>;
}

export function useUpdateStage() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ stage, patch }: StageEdit) =>
      (await apiFetch(`/api/stages/${stage.id}`, stageEnvelopeSchema, { method: 'PATCH', body: patch })).stage,
    optimistic: (bootstrap, { stage, patch }) => ({ ...bootstrap, stages: upsertById(bootstrap.stages, { ...stage, ...patch }) }),
    settle: (bootstrap, stage) => ({ ...bootstrap, stages: upsertById(bootstrap.stages, stage) }),
    failure: ({ stage }) => `Could not update ${stage.name}`,
  });
}

export interface StageOrder {
  readonly pipelineId: string;
  readonly stageIds: readonly string[];
}

export function useReorderStages() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: StageOrder) =>
      (await apiFetch('/api/stages/reorder', stagesEnvelopeSchema, { method: 'POST', body: input })).stages,
    optimistic: (bootstrap, input) => ({
      ...bootstrap,
      stages: bootstrap.stages.map((stage) => {
        const index = input.stageIds.indexOf(stage.id);
        return index === -1 ? stage : { ...stage, sortOrder: index };
      }),
    }),
    settle: (bootstrap, stages) => ({ ...bootstrap, stages: upsertAll(bootstrap.stages, stages) }),
    failure: () => 'Could not reorder the stages',
  });
}

export function useArchiveStage() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: async (stage: StageRow) => (await apiFetch(`/api/stages/${stage.id}`, stageEnvelopeSchema, { method: 'DELETE' })).stage,
    optimistic: (bootstrap, stage) => ({ ...bootstrap, stages: withoutId(bootstrap.stages, stage.id) }),
    settle: (bootstrap, stage) => ({ ...bootstrap, stages: withoutId(bootstrap.stages, stage.id) }),
    failure: (stage) => `Could not archive ${stage.name}`,
  });
}

export interface FieldDraft {
  readonly object: FieldObject;
  readonly pipelineId: string | null;
  readonly key: string;
  readonly label: string;
  readonly type: FieldType;
  readonly options: readonly { readonly value: string; readonly label: string }[];
}

export function useCreateField() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: async (input: FieldDraft) => (await apiFetch('/api/fields', fieldEnvelopeSchema, { method: 'POST', body: input })).field,
    settle: (bootstrap, field) => ({ ...bootstrap, fields: upsertById(bootstrap.fields, field) }),
    failure: (input) => `Could not create ${input.label}`,
  });
}

export interface FieldEdit {
  readonly field: FieldDefinitionRow;
  readonly patch: Partial<Pick<FieldDefinitionRow, 'label' | 'options' | 'description' | 'example'>>;
}

export function useUpdateField() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: async ({ field, patch }: FieldEdit) =>
      (await apiFetch(`/api/fields/${field.id}`, fieldEnvelopeSchema, { method: 'PATCH', body: patch })).field,
    optimistic: (bootstrap, { field, patch }) => ({ ...bootstrap, fields: upsertById(bootstrap.fields, { ...field, ...patch }) }),
    settle: (bootstrap, field) => ({ ...bootstrap, fields: upsertById(bootstrap.fields, field) }),
    failure: ({ field }) => `Could not update ${field.label}`,
  });
}

export function useArchiveField() {
  return useBootstrapMutation({
    mutationFn: async (field: FieldDefinitionRow) => (await apiFetch(`/api/fields/${field.id}`, fieldEnvelopeSchema, { method: 'DELETE' })).field,
    optimistic: (bootstrap, field) => ({ ...bootstrap, fields: withoutId(bootstrap.fields, field.id) }),
    settle: (bootstrap, field) => ({ ...bootstrap, fields: withoutId(bootstrap.fields, field.id) }),
    failure: (field) => `Could not archive ${field.label}`,
  });
}
```

```tsx
'use client';

import Link from 'next/link';
import { usePathname } from 'next/navigation';
import { useWorkspace } from '@/features/workspace/use-workspace.ts';
import { cn } from '@/lib/cn.ts';
import { navRowHover } from '@/lib/interaction.ts';

const ITEMS = [
  { href: '/settings/members', label: 'Members' },
  { href: '/settings/brands', label: 'Brands' },
  { href: '/settings/fields', label: 'Custom fields' },
] as const;

function NavLink({ href, label, indent = false }: { readonly href: string; readonly label: string; readonly indent?: boolean }) {
  const pathname = usePathname();
  const active = pathname === href;
  return (
    <Link
      href={href}
      aria-current={active ? 'page' : undefined}
      className={cn('flex h-7 items-center rounded-md px-2 text-dense', indent && 'pl-6', active ? 'bg-surface-2 text-text' : cn('text-muted', navRowHover))}
    >
      <span className="truncate">{label}</span>
    </Link>
  );
}

export function SettingsNav() {
  const workspace = useWorkspace();
  return (
    <nav aria-label="Settings" className="flex w-56 shrink-0 flex-col gap-0.5 border-border border-r p-3">
      {ITEMS.map((item) => (
        <div key={item.href} className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
          <NavLink href={item.href} label={item.label} />
          {item.href === '/settings/brands'
            ? workspace.pipelines.map((pipeline) => (
                <NavLink
                  key={pipeline.id}
                  indent
                  href={`/settings/pipelines/${pipeline.id}`}
                  label={`${workspace.brandById.get(pipeline.brandId)?.name ?? ''} · ${pipeline.name}`}
                />
              ))
            : null}
        </div>
      ))}
    </nav>
  );
}
```

```tsx
'use client';

import { BRAND_COLORS, type BrandColor } from '@gravity/shared/constants';
import { BRAND_COLOR_CLASS } from '@/features/workspace/brand-color.ts';
import { cn } from '@/lib/cn.ts';
import { swatchLift } from '@/lib/interaction.ts';

export function ColorPicker({ value, onChange, disabled = false }: { readonly value: BrandColor; readonly onChange: (color: BrandColor) => void; readonly disabled?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <fieldset className="flex items-center gap-1.5" disabled={disabled}>
      <legend className="sr-only">Colour</legend>
      {BRAND_COLORS.map((color) => (
        <button
          key={color}
          type="button"
          aria-label={color}
          aria-pressed={color === value}
          onClick={() => onChange(color)}
          className={cn('size-5 rounded-full', BRAND_COLOR_CLASS[color], swatchLift, color === value && 'ring-2 ring-ring ring-offset-1 ring-offset-bg')}
        />
      ))}
    </fieldset>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/settings/brands-panel.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { type BrandColor, DEFAULT_PIPELINE_NAME, PIPELINE_KINDS, type PipelineKind } from '@gravity/shared/constants';
import type { BrandRow } from '@gravity/shared/records';
import { derivePipelineKey } from '@gravity/shared/utils';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { type FormEvent, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input.tsx';
import { EditableField } from '@/features/records/editable-field.tsx';
import { BrandDot } from '@/features/workspace/brand-dot.tsx';
import { useCan } from '@/features/workspace/use-can.ts';
import { useWorkspace } from '@/features/workspace/use-workspace.ts';
import { useArchiveBrand, useCreateBrand, useCreatePipeline, useUpdateBrand } from '@/lib/query/use-config-mutations.ts';
import { ColorPicker } from './color-picker.tsx';

function useTakenKeys(): ReadonlySet<string> {
  const workspace = useWorkspace();
  return useMemo(() => new Set(workspace.pipelines.map((pipeline) => pipeline.key)), [workspace.pipelines]);
}

function NewBrandForm() {
  const create = useCreateBrand();
  const taken = useTakenKeys();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [domain, setDomain] = useState('');
  const [color, setColor] = useState<BrandColor>('blue');
  const [key, setKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const pipelineKey = key ?? (name.trim().length === 0 ? '' : derivePipelineKey(name, taken));
  const submit = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (name.trim().length === 0) return;
    create.mutate({ name: name.trim(), domain: domain.trim() === '' ? null : domain.trim(), color, pipelineKey });
    setName('');
    setDomain('');
    setKey(null);
  };
  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border p-4">
      <h2 className="font-medium text-dense text-text">New brand</h2>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <Input aria-label="Brand name" placeholder="Yodu" value={name} onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)} />
        <Input aria-label="Domain" placeholder="yodu.ai" value={domain} onChange={(event) => setDomain(event.target.value)} />
        <Input aria-label="Pipeline key" placeholder="YOD" value={pipelineKey} onChange={(event) => setKey(event.target.value.toUpperCase())} />
        <ColorPicker value={color} onChange={setColor} />
      </div>
      <Button type="submit" variant="primary" className="self-start">
        Create brand
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}

function NewPipelineForm({ brand }: { readonly brand: BrandRow }) {
  const create = useCreatePipeline();
  const taken = useTakenKeys();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<PipelineKind>('people');
  const key = derivePipelineKey(`${brand.name} ${name}`, taken);
  return (
    <form
      className="flex items-center gap-2"
      onSubmit={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        create.mutate({ brandId: brand.id, name: name.trim() === '' ? DEFAULT_PIPELINE_NAME[kind] : name.trim(), key, kind });
        setName('');
      }}
    >
      <Input aria-label={`New pipeline for ${brand.name}`} placeholder="Pipeline name" value={name} onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)} className="h-7" />
      <select aria-label="Pipeline kind" value={kind} onChange={(event) => setKind(event.target.value === 'deals' ? 'deals' : 'people')} className="h-7 rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-dense">
        {PIPELINE_KINDS.map((entry) => (
          <option key={entry} value={entry}>
            {entry === 'people' ? 'Prospecting' : 'Deals'}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      <Button type="submit" size="sm">
        Add pipeline {key}
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}

function BrandItem({ brand, allowed }: { readonly brand: BrandRow; readonly allowed: boolean }) {
  const workspace = useWorkspace();
  const update = useUpdateBrand();
  const archive = useArchiveBrand();
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState(false);
  const pipelines = workspace.pipelinesOf(brand.id);
  return (
    <li className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border p-4" data-brand={brand.name}>
      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <BrandDot color={brand.color} />
        <span className="font-medium text-text">{brand.name}</span>
        {allowed ? (
          <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" className="ml-auto" onClick={() => setConfirming(true)}>
            Archive
          </Button>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      {allowed ? (
        <>
          <EditableField label="Name" value={brand.name} onSave={(name) => update.mutate({ brand, patch: { name } })} />
          <EditableField label="Domain" value={brand.domain ?? ''} onSave={(domain) => update.mutate({ brand, patch: { domain: domain.trim() === '' ? null : domain.trim() } })} />
          <ColorPicker value={brand.color} onChange={(color) => update.mutate({ brand, patch: { color } })} />
        </>
      ) : null}
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-0.5">
        {pipelines.map((pipeline) => (
          <li key={pipeline.id}>
            <Link href={`/settings/pipelines/${pipeline.id}`} className="text-accent text-dense">
              {pipeline.name} <span className="font-mono text-faint">{pipeline.key}</span>
            </Link>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      {allowed ? <NewPipelineForm brand={brand} /> : null}
      {confirming ? (
        <div role="alertdialog" aria-label={`Archive ${brand.name}`} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-md border border-danger p-2 text-dense">
          <span className="flex-1 text-danger">
            Archive {brand.name}? Its {pipelines.length === 1 ? 'pipeline' : `${pipelines.length} pipelines`} and their leads leave every list.
          </span>
          <Button size="sm" variant="danger" onClick={() => archive.mutate(brand)}>
            Archive
          </Button>
          <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" onClick={() => setConfirming(false)}>
            Keep
          </Button>
        </div>
      ) : null}
    </li>
  );
}

export function BrandsPanel() {
  const workspace = useWorkspace();
  const allowed = useCan('pipeline:manage');
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-3xl flex-col gap-6 px-6 py-6">
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <h1 className="font-medium text-lg text-text">Brands</h1>
        <p className="text-muted text-dense">Each brand has its own pipelines, stages and playbook.</p>
      </header>
      {allowed ? <NewBrandForm /> : <p className="text-muted text-dense">Changing brands needs the member role. Ask an admin.</p>}
      <ul className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        {workspace.brands.map((brand) => (
          <BrandItem key={brand.id} brand={brand} allowed={allowed} />
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/settings/pipeline-panel.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { STAGE_CATEGORIES, STAGE_CATEGORY_LABELS, type StageCategory } from '@gravity/shared/constants';
import type { StageRow } from '@gravity/shared/records';
import Link from 'next/link';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button.tsx';
import { EmptyState } from '@/components/ui/empty-state.tsx';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input.tsx';
import { StageGlyph } from '@/features/leads/lead-glyphs.tsx';
import { EditableField } from '@/features/records/editable-field.tsx';
import { useCan } from '@/features/workspace/use-can.ts';
import { useWorkspace } from '@/features/workspace/use-workspace.ts';
import {
  useArchivePipeline,
  useArchiveStage,
  useCreateStage,
  useReorderStages,
  useUpdatePipeline,
  useUpdateStage,
} from '@/lib/query/use-config-mutations.ts';
import { movedIds } from './field-key.ts';

function asCategory(value: string): StageCategory {
  return STAGE_CATEGORIES.find((category) => category === value) ?? 'open';
}

function StageItem({ stage, index, count, allowed, onMove }: { readonly stage: StageRow; readonly index: number; readonly count: number; readonly allowed: boolean; readonly onMove: (direction: -1 | 1) => void }) {
  const update = useUpdateStage();
  const archive = useArchiveStage();
  return (
    <li data-stage={stage.name} className="flex items-center gap-2 border-border border-b py-1.5 text-dense">
      <StageGlyph category={stage.category} label={stage.name} />
      <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
        {allowed ? (
          <EditableField label="Stage" value={stage.name} onSave={(name) => update.mutate({ stage, patch: { name } })} />
        ) : (
          <span className="text-text">{stage.name}</span>
        )}
      </div>
      <select
        aria-label={`Type of ${stage.name}`}
        disabled={!allowed}
        value={stage.category}
        onChange={(event) => update.mutate({ stage, patch: { category: asCategory(event.target.value) } })}
        className="h-7 rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-2"
      >
        {STAGE_CATEGORIES.map((category) => (
          <option key={category} value={category}>
            {STAGE_CATEGORY_LABELS[category]}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      {allowed ? (
        <>
          <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Move ${stage.name} up`} disabled={index === 0} onClick={() => onMove(-1)}>
            Up
          </Button>
          <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Move ${stage.name} down`} disabled={index === count - 1} onClick={() => onMove(1)}>
            Down
          </Button>
          <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Archive ${stage.name}`} onClick={() => archive.mutate(stage)}>
            Archive
          </Button>
        </>
      ) : null}
    </li>
  );
}

function NewStageForm({ pipelineId }: { readonly pipelineId: string }) {
  const create = useCreateStage();
  const [name, setName] = useState('');
  const [category, setCategory] = useState<StageCategory>('open');
  return (
    <form
      className="flex items-center gap-2"
      onSubmit={(event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (name.trim().length === 0) return;
        create.mutate({ pipelineId, name: name.trim(), category });
        setName('');
      }}
    >
      <Input aria-label="New stage" placeholder="Nurture" value={name} onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)} className="h-7" />
      <select aria-label="New stage type" value={category} onChange={(event) => setCategory(asCategory(event.target.value))} className="h-7 rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-dense">
        {STAGE_CATEGORIES.map((entry) => (
          <option key={entry} value={entry}>
            {STAGE_CATEGORY_LABELS[entry]}
          </option>
        ))}
      </select>
      <Button type="submit" size="sm">
        Add stage
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}

export function PipelinePanel({ pipelineId }: { readonly pipelineId: string }) {
  const workspace = useWorkspace();
  const allowed = useCan('pipeline:manage');
  const updatePipeline = useUpdatePipeline();
  const archivePipeline = useArchivePipeline();
  const reorder = useReorderStages();
  const pipeline = workspace.pipelineById.get(pipelineId);
  if (!workspace.ready) return null;
  if (pipeline === undefined) {
    return <EmptyState title="This pipeline does not exist" description="It may have been archived." action={<Link href="/settings/brands">Back to brands</Link>} />;
  }
  const stages = workspace.stagesOf(pipeline.id);
  const ids = stages.map((stage) => stage.id);
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-3xl flex-col gap-6 px-6 py-6">
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <h1 className="font-medium text-lg text-text">
          {workspace.brandById.get(pipeline.brandId)?.name ?? ''} · {pipeline.name}
        </h1>
        <p className="text-muted text-dense">Leads in this pipeline are numbered {pipeline.key}-1, {pipeline.key}-2 and so on.</p>
      </header>
      {allowed ? (
        <div className="flex flex-col">
          <EditableField label="Name" value={pipeline.name} onSave={(name) => updatePipeline.mutate({ pipeline, patch: { name } })} />
          <EditableField label="Key" value={pipeline.key} onSave={(key) => updatePipeline.mutate({ pipeline, patch: { key: key.trim().toUpperCase() } })} />
        </div>
      ) : (
        <p className="text-muted text-dense">Changing pipelines needs the member role. Ask an admin.</p>
      )}
      <section className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <h2 className="font-medium text-dense text-text">Stages</h2>
        <ol aria-label="Stages" className="flex flex-col">
          {stages.map((stage, index) => (
            <StageItem
              key={stage.id}
              stage={stage}
              index={index}
              count={stages.length}
              allowed={allowed}
              onMove={(direction) => reorder.mutate({ pipelineId: pipeline.id, stageIds: movedIds(ids, index, direction) })}
            />
          ))}
        </ol>
        {allowed ? <NewStageForm pipelineId={pipeline.id} /> : null}
      </section>
      {allowed ? (
        <Button variant="danger" size="sm" className="self-start" onClick={() => archivePipeline.mutate(pipeline)}>
          Archive pipeline
        </Button>
      ) : null}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`apps/web/src/features/settings/fields-panel.tsx`:

```tsx
'use client';

import { FIELD_TYPE_LABELS, FIELD_TYPES, type FieldType, SELECT_FIELD_TYPES } from '@gravity/shared/constants';
import { type FormEvent, useState } from 'react';
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button.tsx';
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input.tsx';
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea.tsx';
import { EditableField } from '@/features/records/editable-field.tsx';
import { useCan } from '@/features/workspace/use-can.ts';
import { useWorkspace } from '@/features/workspace/use-workspace.ts';
import { useArchiveField, useCreateField, useUpdateField } from '@/lib/query/use-config-mutations.ts';
import { fieldKeyFromLabel, optionsFromLines } from './field-key.ts';

const OBJECTS = [
  { value: 'lead', label: 'Leads' },
  { value: 'person', label: 'People' },
  { value: 'company', label: 'Companies' },
] as const;

type EditableObject = (typeof OBJECTS)[number]['value'];

function asFieldType(value: string): FieldType {
  return FIELD_TYPES.find((type) => type === value) ?? 'text';
}

function NewFieldForm({ object }: { readonly object: EditableObject }) {
  const workspace = useWorkspace();
  const create = useCreateField();
  const [label, setLabel] = useState('');
  const [key, setKey] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [type, setType] = useState<FieldType>('text');
  const [optionsText, setOptionsText] = useState('');
  const [pipelineId, setPipelineId] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const derivedKey = key ?? fieldKeyFromLabel(label);
  const choice = SELECT_FIELD_TYPES.includes(type);

  const submit = (event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) => {
    event.preventDefault();
    const options = choice ? optionsFromLines(optionsText) : [];
    if (label.trim().length === 0) {
      setError('Name the field.');
      return;
    }
    if (choice && options.length === 0) {
      setError('Add at least one option.');
      return;
    }
    setError(null);
    create.mutate({ object, pipelineId: object === 'lead' && pipelineId !== '' ? pipelineId : null, key: derivedKey, label: label.trim(), type, options });
    setLabel('');
    setKey(null);
    setOptionsText('');
  };

  return (
    <form onSubmit={submit} className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border border-border p-4">
      <h2 className="font-medium text-dense text-text">New field</h2>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
        <Input aria-label="Field name" placeholder="Deal size" value={label} onChange={(event) => setLabel(event.target.value)} />
        <Input aria-label="Key" value={derivedKey} onChange={(event) => setKey(event.target.value)} className="font-mono" />
        <select aria-label="Type" value={type} onChange={(event) => setType(asFieldType(event.target.value))} className="h-9 rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-dense">
          {FIELD_TYPES.map((entry) => (
            <option key={entry} value={entry}>
              {FIELD_TYPE_LABELS[entry]}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        {object === 'lead' ? (
          <select aria-label="Pipeline" value={pipelineId} onChange={(event) => setPipelineId(event.target.value)} className="h-9 rounded-md border border-border bg-surface px-2 text-dense">
            <option value="">All pipelines</option>
            {workspace.pipelines.map((pipeline) => (
              <option key={pipeline.id} value={pipeline.id}>
                {pipeline.name} ({pipeline.key})
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        ) : null}
      </div>
      {choice ? (
        <Textarea aria-label="Options, one per line" rows={3} value={optionsText} onChange={(event) => setOptionsText(event.target.value)} />
      ) : null}
      {error === null ? null : (
        <p role="alert" className="text-danger text-xs">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      <Button type="submit" variant="primary" className="self-start">
        Create field
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}

export function FieldsPanel() {
  const workspace = useWorkspace();
  const allowed = useCan('field:manage');
  const update = useUpdateField();
  const archive = useArchiveField();
  const [object, setObject] = useState<EditableObject>('lead');
  const fields = workspace.allFields.filter((field) => field.object === object);
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex w-full max-w-3xl flex-col gap-6 px-6 py-6">
      <header className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <h1 className="font-medium text-lg text-text">Custom fields</h1>
        <p className="text-muted text-dense">Fields are validated on every write, by people and by agents.</p>
      </header>
      <div role="group" aria-label="Record type" className="flex gap-1">
        {OBJECTS.map((entry) => (
          <Button key={entry.value} size="sm" variant={entry.value === object ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'} aria-pressed={entry.value === object} onClick={() => setObject(entry.value)}>
            {entry.label}
          </Button>
        ))}
      </div>
      {allowed ? <NewFieldForm object={object} /> : <p className="text-muted text-dense">Changing fields needs the member role. Ask an admin.</p>}
      <ul className="flex flex-col">
        {fields.map((field) => (
          <li key={field.id} className="flex items-center gap-3 border-border border-b py-1.5 text-dense">
            <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
              {allowed ? <EditableField label="Label" value={field.label} onSave={(label) => update.mutate({ field, patch: { label } })} /> : field.label}
            </div>
            <span className="font-mono text-faint text-xs">{field.key}</span>
            <span className="w-28 text-muted">{FIELD_TYPE_LABELS[field.type]}</span>
            <span className="w-32 truncate text-faint">{field.pipelineId === null ? 'All pipelines' : (workspace.pipelineById.get(field.pipelineId)?.name ?? '')}</span>
            {allowed ? (
              <Button size="sm" variant="ghost" aria-label={`Archive ${field.label}`} onClick={() => archive.mutate(field)}>
                Archive
              </Button>
            ) : null}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </div>
  );
}
```

### Task 21: Playwright realtime smoke test

Ports Orbit's end-to-end harness: `$ORBIT_SRC/apps/web/playwright.config.ts`, `$ORBIT_SRC/apps/web/e2e/base-url.ts`, `$ORBIT_SRC/apps/web/e2e/global-setup.ts` and the two-context pattern of `$ORBIT_SRC/apps/web/e2e/second-workspace-realtime.spec.ts`. Gravity has no seed yet (M0.3), so the global setup creates a fresh workspace with two members and a brand through core services and writes a fixture file the spec reads. Like Orbit, it runs against a running dev server and is not part of `bun run verify`.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/web/package.json` (devDependency `"@playwright/test": "^1.62.1"`, script `"test:e2e": "bun --env-file=../../.env --bun playwright test"`), `package.json` (script `"test:e2e": "bun run --filter '@gravity/web' test:e2e"`), `.gitignore`
- Create: `apps/web/playwright.config.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/base-url.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/fixture.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/global-setup.ts`, `apps/web/e2e/realtime-lead.spec.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createUser` (`@gravity/core/test-support`), `createOrganization`, `resolvePrincipal`, `createInvite`, `acceptInvite`, `createBrand` (`@gravity/core`); the dev sign-in route `POST /api/dev/sign-in` (M0.1); quick create (Task 19); the `lead-row-*` test ids (Task 14).
- Produces: `E2EFixture = { ownerEmail; teammateEmail; brandName; pipelineKey; pipelineId }`, `readFixture()`, `writeFixture(fixture)`, `BASE`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Install Playwright and its browser**

```bash
cd apps/web && bun add -d @playwright/test@^1.62.1 && bunx playwright install chromium && cd ../..
printf '\napps/web/e2e/.fixture.json\napps/web/test-results/\napps/web/playwright-report/\n' >> .gitignore
```

Expected: `@playwright/test` in `apps/web/package.json` devDependencies; Chromium downloaded; the three ignore lines appended. Add the two `test:e2e` scripts listed above.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the harness**

`apps/web/e2e/base-url.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from 'zod';

const baseUrlSchema = z
  .url()
  .transform((value) => value.replace(/\/+$/, ''))
  .catch('http://localhost:3300');

export const BASE = baseUrlSchema.parse(process.env['GRAVITY_E2E_BASE_URL'] ?? process.env['NEXT_PUBLIC_APP_URL']);
```

`apps/web/e2e/fixture.ts`:

```ts
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { z } from 'zod';

const FIXTURE_PATH = resolve(import.meta.dirname, '.fixture.json');

export const e2eFixtureSchema = z.object({
  ownerEmail: z.string().email(),
  teammateEmail: z.string().email(),
  brandName: z.string().min(1),
  pipelineKey: z.string().min(2),
  pipelineId: z.string().min(1),
});
export type E2EFixture = z.infer<typeof e2eFixtureSchema>;

export function writeFixture(fixture: E2EFixture): void {
  writeFileSync(FIXTURE_PATH, JSON.stringify(fixture, null, 2));
}

export function readFixture(): E2EFixture {
  return e2eFixtureSchema.parse(JSON.parse(readFileSync(FIXTURE_PATH, 'utf8')));
}
```

`apps/web/e2e/global-setup.ts`:

```ts
import { acceptInvite, createBrand, createInvite, createOrganization, resolvePrincipal } from '@gravity/core';
import { createUser } from '@gravity/core/test-support';
import { writeFixture } from './fixture.ts';

export default async function globalSetup(): Promise<void> {
  const suffix = Date.now().toString(36);
  const owner = await createUser(`E2E Owner ${suffix}`);
  const teammate = await createUser(`E2E Teammate ${suffix}`);
  const workspace = await createOrganization(owner.id, { name: `E2E ${suffix}`, slug: `e2e-${suffix}` });
  const admin = await resolvePrincipal(owner.id, workspace.organization.id);
  const { token } = await createInvite(admin, { email: teammate.email, role: 'member' });
  await acceptInvite(token, teammate.id);
  const brandName = `Nimbus ${suffix}`;
  const brand = await createBrand({ principal: admin }, { name: brandName });
  writeFixture({
    ownerEmail: owner.email,
    teammateEmail: teammate.email,
    brandName,
    pipelineKey: brand.pipeline.key,
    pipelineId: brand.pipeline.id,
  });
}
```

`createUser` only inserts a user row; it does not truncate anything, so it is safe against the development database (unlike `resetDatabase`, which refuses any database not named `gravity_test*`). The users are new on every run, so each belongs only to the fresh workspace and the dev sign-in lands in it.

`apps/web/playwright.config.ts`:

```ts
import { defineConfig, devices } from '@playwright/test';

const { BASE } = await import('./e2e/base-url.ts');

export default defineConfig({
  testDir: './e2e',
  globalSetup: './e2e/global-setup.ts',
  timeout: 120_000,
  expect: { timeout: 30_000 },
  fullyParallel: false,
  workers: 1,
  reporter: [['list']],
  use: {
    baseURL: BASE,
    trace: 'retain-on-failure',
    ...devices['Desktop Chrome'],
    viewport: { width: 1440, height: 900 },
  },
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the spec**

`apps/web/e2e/realtime-lead.spec.ts`:

```ts
import { type BrowserContext, expect, type Page, test } from '@playwright/test';
import { BASE } from './base-url.ts';
import { readFixture } from './fixture.ts';

const PROPAGATION_BUDGET_MS = 500;

async function signIn(context: BrowserContext, email: string): Promise<Page> {
  const response = await context.request.post(`${BASE}/api/dev/sign-in`, { data: { email } });
  expect(response.ok()).toBe(true);
  return await context.newPage();
}

function leadRow(page: Page, name: string) {
  return page.locator('[data-testid^="lead-row-"]', { hasText: name });
}

test('a lead created in one browser appears in another within 500ms', async ({ browser }) => {
  const fixture = readFixture();
  const owner = await signIn(await browser.newContext(), fixture.ownerEmail);
  const teammate = await signIn(await browser.newContext(), fixture.teammateEmail);
  const listUrl = `${BASE}/leads/${fixture.pipelineKey}`;
  await Promise.all([owner.goto(listUrl), teammate.goto(listUrl)]);
  const emptyCopy = `No leads in ${fixture.brandName} · Prospecting yet.`;
  await expect(owner.getByText(emptyCopy)).toBeVisible();
  await expect(teammate.getByText(emptyCopy)).toBeVisible();

  const warmUp = `Warm up ${Date.now()}`;
  const warmed = await owner.request.post(`${BASE}/api/leads/quick`, {
    data: { pipelineId: fixture.pipelineId, person: { name: warmUp } },
  });
  expect(warmed.ok()).toBe(true);
  await expect(leadRow(teammate, warmUp)).toBeVisible();

  const name = `Realtime ${Date.now()}`;
  await owner.keyboard.press('c');
  await owner.getByLabel('Person').fill(name);
  const created = owner.waitForResponse((response) => response.url().endsWith('/api/leads/quick') && response.ok());
  await owner.keyboard.press('ControlOrMeta+Enter');
  await expect(leadRow(owner, name)).toBeVisible();
  await created;
  const answeredAt = Date.now();
  await expect(leadRow(teammate, name)).toBeVisible({ timeout: PROPAGATION_BUDGET_MS });
  expect(Date.now() - answeredAt).toBeLessThan(PROPAGATION_BUDGET_MS);
});
```

The warm-up lead proves both pages hold a subscribed socket before the measured create, so the 500ms window measures propagation, not connection set-up. The clock starts when the owner's create response arrives, which is when the route has committed and published the outbox rows.


Expected: `1 passed`. If the teammate never sees the warm-up lead, the realtime host is not running or Redis is down: check `apps/realtime` logs for `listening on 3400` and `docker ps` for `gravity-redis`. If it appears but after more than 500ms, open the trace (`apps/web/test-results/`) and compare the `delta` frame time in the teammate's websocket with the owner's response time.

| Field definitions with generated Zod validation; human edits beat agents | 1, 5, 6 |
| Company upsert by domain; person upsert by email or LinkedIn provider id or URL, dedupe; employment | 6 |
| Leads: next number, stage, owner, priority, next action, hold, close, bulk | 7 |

| Verbs `S`/`A`/`P`/`N`/`Shift+H`/`Cmd+Backspace`, anchored pickers, bulk with Undo, `Cmd+Z` | 15 |
| `F` filter menu, `/` search, `Shift+F`, `Cmd+S` save view, Views in the sidebar | 16 |

| Context panel 360 to 640px, remembered per user, `]` | 13 |

Review Focus tests are placed: filter parity in Task 8 (`filter-sql.test.ts`), duplicate open lead and foreign ids in Task 7 (`lead-service.test.ts`), out-of-order, echo and list moves in Task 12 (`crm-deltas.test.ts`), bootstrap ETag after removals in Task 10 (`bootstrap/route.test.ts`).

Decisions a reviewer should know:

- The partial unique index on open leads cannot reference the stage table, so `lead.stage_category` is denormalized and kept in step by the lead service; a stage's category cannot change while leads sit in it (Task 5), which keeps the copy correct without a fan-out rewrite.
- The bootstrap ETag fingerprints live ids and sync ids instead of Orbit's per-table maximum sync id, because a maximum does not move on delete or archive.
- Brand colours are eight token names mapped onto existing Orbit tokens (`accent`, `success`, `warning`, `danger`, `merged`, `presence`, `priority-high`, `faint`); no token value is added or edited.
- Leads, people and companies are fetched in full pages of 500 and 200 and filtered, grouped and sorted on the client, so a delta can place a row in every open list without a refetch; very large pipelines are the M0.3 performance task's concern.
- Quick create and saved views send client-generated UUIDv7 ids so the optimistic row and the server row share an id; brand and pipeline creation write the bootstrap from the response because their children only exist on the server.
